**What T150 built (D-2008 to D-2012).** EMERGENCY card and pick emails, the "Emergency emails at any hour" switch with its test emergency email, and the after-hours tick's conditions (CARD-021, BIZ-007, BIZ-037, BIZ-042, INT-033, PICK-044). `migrations/0147_emergency_emails.sql`: `businesses.emergency_switch` (per member: switch, test sent, last Yes; no address, no DELETE) and `businesses.business.after_hours_suspended_at`. `app/businesses/src/emergency_switch.rs`: `set_emergency_emails`, `send_emergency_test` (`businesses.emergency_test` task), `record_alert_answer` (a Yes needs a test sent since the switch went on, as switching off clears both; a Yes lasts `EMERGENCY_YES_LIFE`, 90 days; only an owner's or "Confirm and billing" person's Yes ends a suspension), `covered`, `set_after_hours` (refused unless a current owner or "Confirm and billing" person has the switch on and a current Yes), `recheck_after_hours` and `switch_off_warns` (the last switch off, a No or a removal unticks and queues `businesses.after_hours_off` to the owner with `reason` `removed`; a suspension sends it with `suspended`), `queue_emergency_retests`, `missed_in_a_row` and `suspend_after_hours` (two unvisited night cards; a new Yes restores), `night_people`, and `night_count` (exported as `covered_night_count`: INT-033's narrowed count). `app/quotes/src/emergency_card_notice.rs`: `queue_emergency_card` (EMERGENCY subject and first line; by day all at once, at night the switched-on people at once and the rest at 8 a.m.). `app/picks/src/emergency_emails.rs`: `emergency_pick_plan`, `emergency_pick_subject`. `app/portals/src/emergency_switch_page.rs` with `templates/emergency_test_email.html`: the switch page and its POST (route not served). Not wired (D-2010, D-2011): `api::night_count` still counts every ticked business; `messaging`'s sender still holds every notice at night and ignores the payload's `subject`; no handler turns `intake.emergency_released` into cards; no sender for the two new task kinds. Tests: `tests/integration/tests/emergency_card_email.rs`.

**What T254 built (D-2013 to D-2016).** The production release (PICK-022, PICK-023, PRIV-005, BIZ-014, CALL-001; D-1563, D-1564, D-1688, D-1739, D-1740). `app/picks/src/settle.rs`: `picks::api::settle(tx, &Releasable)` is the only code that moves a pick to `paid` or `free`; it takes `payments`' cleared `Releasable` (private fields, no `Clone`), never a bare basis, locks the pick row, moves only an unended `awaiting_confirm` pick (one audit row `picks.settled_paid` or `picks.settled_free`) and answers `Settled::Moved(job)`, `Was(job)` (already `paid` or `free` for that basis) or `Left` (unknown, ended or any other status, nothing to release). `app/server/src/release_sink.rs`: `PickReleaser` is `payments`' `ReleaseSink`: settle and commit, then for `Moved` or `Was` find the homeowner's contact with `intake::api::job_homeowner` (none: one High `picks.release_no_contact` alert, nothing released), mint with `server::release_minter()` and call `picks::api::release` with the vault and the masked pool, `email_chosen` false until the PICK-018 choice is stored (D-1740); `Already`, `MovedOn` and `WaitingForAddress` are success, any failure `ReleaseError::Database` (the route's 500, so Stripe resends; the release row keeps it once). The same sink takes `no_fee_release`'s `Releasable`. `release_sink::stripe_webhook(settings, pool, pairs)` builds the route's state from `ASKHAVA_STRIPE_WEBHOOK_SECRET` (unset: no route in dev and CI, an error in test and live), `ASKHAVA_STRIPE_SECRET_KEY`, `ASKHAVA_STRIPE_ACCOUNT_ID`, `ASKHAVA_ADAPTER_MODE_STRIPE` (`live` only in live), `ASKHAVA_STRIPE_BASE_URL` (default the stand-in's `/stripe` or `https://api.stripe.com`, `check_base_url`), the vault keys, the Twilio settings (the masked pool) and the SEC-040 client-address policy; `server::build_router` merges `stripe_webhook_routes` with it and `main` serves with `into_make_service_with_connect_info::<SocketAddr>()`. The masked-line retry (D-1688): `release` queues one `picks.line_retry` task (dedupe `picks.line_retry.<pick>`) when a shown release's line fails to open (with the High `picks.release_line_failed` alert, one transaction) and also when the release itself errs after its claim (a vault read failing once the release row stands), so that release's line still opens; an error before the claim queues nothing, so the per-pick key is not spent (D-2017); `picks::api::retry_line`, registered in `app/server/src/bin/worker.rs` over the masked pool from the Twilio settings (required in test and live), opens the line from the release row's bound contact only while the pick is still `paid` or `free` and `calls::api::job_had_line` (new, any line open or closed) is false, so a line closed after 30 days is never reopened; a failure is the task's error (worker retries, dead letter). Tests: `app/server/tests/release_recovery.rs` (TD-PICK-49, TD-PICK-50) through the sink with real proofs, the signed webhook with a fake Stripe client, a vault that cannot read then can, new pools for restarts and a held pool lock for the line failure; every row is made through the module APIs, from the business and the quote to the page pick and the charge (D-2013). Not built: the job's own move to Payment pending or Connected at the release (D-2015); a settled pick whose job has no homeowner request is `MovedOn` with one High `picks.release_no_contact` alert and nothing retries it (D-2017).

**What T262 built (D-2091 to D-2095).** Stored PINs, stored PIN-try counts and the inbound voice route (CALL-002 to CALL-005, CALL-025, SEC-016, SEC-017, FEE-022; D-1684, D-1685, D-1708, D-1755, D-1836). `migrations/0151_calls_pins_attempts.sql`: `calls.number_pool.pin` (four digits from the OS random source, a partial unique index on (pool number, PIN) of open lines; lines from before keep none), `calls.pin_try` (an HMAC-SHA-256 digest of the calling number under `ID_SECRET` with label `pin-try|v1` and its key version, the pool number, the Arizona day and `tries`, at most 6; app role UPDATE of `tries` only), `calls.pin_call` (append-only, one row per CallSid and try with the day's count it was judged on) and `calls.call_start` (parent CallSid, line, start, the dialed leg's SID that T271 fills; UPDATE of `dialed_sid` only). `app/calls/src/pins.rs`: `assign_number` draws the PIN under the pool lock (at most `PIN_DRAWS` = 8 draws, then the High `calls.pool_exhausted` alert is committed and the line refused with `CallsError::NoFreePin`); `stored_pin`, `voice_turn`, `voice_confirm`, `pin_tries_today`, `call_start`, `caller_digest`, `arizona_day`. `answer_call` routes on the stored PINs only. A wrong try is counted by one upsert capped at 6 inside a transaction that also records (CallSid, try), so a repeated callback rolls its count back and answers the same step. `app/server/src/inbound_voice.rs`: `POST /webhooks/twilio/voice` (`VOICE_PATH`), signature-checked like `inbound_text` and built by `voice_hooks` from the same `Webhooks`, the masked pool and the PIN wait; the signed query (`try=N`, `confirm=N`) carries the turn, never a line ID; replies are `calls::api` TwiML (prompt, read-back on A attestation, dial, hang-up), and the dial stores the call's start once per CallSid. The calling-number digest is keyed by `ID_SECRET` version 1 and is listed under `ID_SECRET` for the PRIV-024 key inventory (D-2094). Gaps: `job_pin` (the ID modulo 10,000) stayed public for `request_link` and the TD-CALL-02 pure tests, which T262 did not own (D-2091), until T326 moved them to the stored or an explicit PIN and deleted it (D-2247, D-3129); a homeowner calling her own line is not dialled, since no business phone number is stored where `calls` can read it (D-2093); `gate` (CALL-012) is not applied, as staff-phone approvals are not stored yet (T148, D-2093).

**What T311 built (D-2201, D-2491).** The voice read-back confirm connects only the line read back (CALL-005, CALL-002). `migrations/0174_calls_readback_line.sql`: nullable `calls.pin_call.readback_line` (the read-back line's ID, try 2 only), written by the insert that records the try, so no `UPDATE` grant. `app/calls/src/pins.rs`: a read-back turn, and Twilio's resend of it, reads back the stored line while it is still open on the pool number and the caller's business's (else the PIN prompt); `voice_confirm` connects that stored line under the same test and otherwise answers as an unconfirmed press or hangs up, never another line. The confirm URL still carries only the try number.

**What T282 built (D-2129).** Each call's start reaches FEE-022 (CALL-007, CALL-008, TXT-026; D-1836). `app/calls/src/handlers.rs`: `call_start_confirmed(db, line, parent, dialed, at)` joins the dialed leg's SID to the parent's `calls.call_start` row (or creates it at `at` when the voice webhook's write was lost) and never moves a stored start; `call_log` left-joins that row by dialed SID and line and returns `LoggedCall::started_at`. `picks`' `call_record` uses the start, or the end when none was recorded (the call still counts). Gap: `dial_twiml` does not yet request the dialed leg's status callbacks, because TD-CALL-03's test pins its exact tags and T282 does not own it; T271 or a harness task adds them with that test (D-2129).

**What T270 built (D-2145 to D-2147).** The unreachable refund and the fraud warning close only on a succeeded refund, and a free pick ends as a no-show too (FEE-022, FEE-026, FEE-035, FEE-052, JOB-013, JOB-027; audit B07, B08, D-1836). `app/payments/src/refund_state.rs`: `payments::api::refund_state(conn, pick)` reads the pick's `payments.refund` row and whether its charge has a `payments.dispute` row as a `PickRefundState` (named apart from T258's `RefundState`, D-2145): no row `NoRefund`, `sending` or `pending` `InFlight`, `succeeded` `Succeeded`, `failed` or `canceled` `Failed`, `blocked` (or a dispute on a refund that would still send, as `RefundStatus::next` judges it) `Blocked`; an unreadable stored status is `Failed`. `app/picks/src/unreachable_refund.rs`: `decide_at_96_hours` and `fraud_warning` read it after `request_refund`/`early_fraud_warning` and close, move, end or email only on `Succeeded`; an in-flight try gives the new `AtNinetySix::RefundInFlight` (and `fraud_warning` `false`), so a second run while Stripe has not answered closes and emails nothing (D-2146). `no_call_at_72_hours` ends a `paid` or `free` pick as `no_show` with its ended time in the transaction of the move and the line close; a pick in neither rolls all three back and answers `PicksError::MovedOn`; `fraud_warning` still ends only a `paid` pick, the same way (D-2147). `RefundOutcome` and the refund state machine are unchanged. Tests: `tests/integration/tests/unreachable_refund_states.rs` (TD-FEE-47).

**What T279 built (D-2185 to D-2189).** The texting switch and a number suspension are stored, an emergency while texting is off is handled, and the WEB-007 banner turns on (TXT-021 to TXT-024, INT-004, WEB-007; D-1807, D-1843). `migrations/0169_messaging_mode_state.sql`: `messaging.mode_state`, append-only (app role SELECT and INSERT): one whole row per change and audience (`texting_on`, `line_state` working/suspended/restored with `suspended_since`, `launch_channel` sms/web_and_email, Casey's `actor_id`, `changed_at`); the latest row is the line, none means homeowner on and business off. `app/messaging/src/mode_state.rs`: `load_lines` (with the configured numbers the caller passes), `launch_channel`, and T108's controls `switch_on` (refused while suspended, D-144), `switch_off`, `record_suspension`, `record_restore`, `set_launch_channel`, each under one advisory lock with its `messaging.mode_changed` audit row, writing nothing when nothing changes. Casey records a suspension, since Twilio announces it by email and console; no detection from send refusals is built (D-2185). `migrations/0170_ops_status_banner.sql` and `app/ops/src/status_banner.rs`: `ops.status_banner`, append-only, the latest row is the banner (none: hidden); `show_banner` (`BannerBy::System(kind, id)` or `Owner`), `clear_banner` (Casey only; the table's check refuses a hidden row by the system), `banner_shown` for T101's layout. `app/server/src/inbound_text.rs`: after the opt-out and HELP steps, a homeowner's 911-list text while her line is paused (switched off, suspended, restored and waiting, or `ops`' `texting` or `all` pause on) runs `emergency_plan` and `raise_emergency` (one critical alert per message), shows the banner, queues a `messaging.paused_safety` task and asks intake's safety call only where the plan keeps it; intake's step runs as before, and the texts intake still queues stay queued, refused by `send_on_line` (D-2187, D-2188); an opted-out or listed homeowner gets the banner and the follow-up but no second critical alert and never a call (D-2189). The task's handler `run_paused_safety` reads her email on a bare connection (the vault refuses a read inside the delivery's transaction) and queues the INT-004 text by email through the existing text-by-email notice, or raises a High `messaging.call_back_due` alert on her contact ID for Casey's call list (TXT-023, PRIV-006), once per message (D-2186). The worker registers it with `SafetySender::call_list_only()`, so until a footer postal setting exists every follow-up is a call-list entry (D-2186). Not wired yet: the email path in the worker, the owner-portal pages (T108) and the layout's banner (T101). Tests: `tests/integration/tests/suspension_mode.rs` (TD-TXT-33).

**What T294 built (D-2486 to D-2488).** The pages take the brand from the brand file (WORD-001, WEB-001, SEC-025; D-1932). `app/site/templates/base.html` (title suffix, home link) and the portals pages `quote_page.html`, `pick_review.html`, `confirm_pay.html`, `why_charged.html`, `pick_code.html`, `notice_settings.html` and `feedback.html` call `askhava_core::api::brand()` from the template, so no handler passes it; the result page's `data:` contact card encodes the names and domain with Askama's `urlencode_strict`. In `portals`, `feedback::share_label`, `business_signup::notice_on`, the `why_charged` on-site lead line, `business_auth`'s emailed sign-in site (`https://<domain>`) and `owner_auth`'s passkey relying-party name and "<name> owner" read `brand()`. Output is byte-identical under the embedded brand (`app/portals/src/feedback.rs` `brand_tests`, TD-WORD-37).

**What T295 built (D-2833 to D-2836).** The last brand literals left the code (WORD-001, WORD-008, FEE-049; D-1932): `payments::api::statement_prefix()` and `IntentText::description` read the brand file, `ai`'s instruction text is a `BrandedText` filled with the brand when sent, `output_checks` lets the brand's name words stay capitalised, `intake`'s question text opens `<short_name>:` and its quote-page link is on the brand's domain, and `picks::api::terms_line` is built once from the brand. `tests/docs/test_brand_literals.py` is strict (no `tests/brand_literal_baseline.json`), and `tests/integration/tests/brand_setting.rs` (TD-WORD-38) installs a fake brand and checks every channel, the legal records' names and the `AUTHORIZATION_TEXT` hash per version. With AI off, `keyword_answer` skips no question whose option follows a negation within three words (INT-039, D-2638).

**What T155 built (D-2507 to D-2511).** Sign-up capability setup (BIZ-049, BIZ-004, CARD-001; D-393, D-492). `app/businesses/src/capability.rs`: `checklist_offer` (the license map's job types for the classes, pre-ticked, then the no-license extras, by trade), `pre_ticked`, `kept_ticks`, `is_recurring`, `LargestJob`, `Capability`, `CapabilityAnswers`, `save_capability` (ticks only from the offer, towns, after-hours tick and the `businesses.capability` row in one transaction; `migrations/0150_businesses_capability.sql`) and `capabilities`. `app/quotes/src/capability_match.rs`: `JobFit`, `CapabilitySkip`, `capability_skip`, `capable_pool` (drop skipped businesses before `rules::match_job`), `band_floor_cents`. `portals::business_signup` adds `CAPABILITY_ROUTE`, `CapabilityStep` (one page or guided), `CapabilityValues`, `CapabilityProblem`, `capability_summary` and `capability_page` over `templates/capability_setup.html`; `portals::business_actions` adds `quote_top_cents`, `LargestWarning`, `largest_job_warning` and `quote_form_page_warned` (the confirmation is the warned cents, D-2510). Not wired yet (D-2508): the card send's call to `capable_pool`, the served route, the quote POST's warning check. Tests: `tests/integration/tests/capability_setup.rs`, `tests/e2e/capability_setup.spec.ts` (TD-BIZ-29).

**What T207 built (D-2592, D-2593, D-2595, D-2596).** Re-applying the license map when a daily list changes a class (BIZ-050, LIC-043; D-393). `app/businesses/src/capability.rs`: `license_change` (pure: ticked job types the old classes covered and the new classes no longer cover, so no-license and agency-licensed job types are kept, and job types of switched-on categories only the new classes cover that are not ticked), `reapply_license` (one transaction, business row locked first: removes those ticks with a `job_types` audit row, pauses through `pause_for_rule` with `no_covered_job_type` when nothing is left ticked and the business was active, queues one `businesses.license_change` task naming the removed and offered job types and the dropped classes, deduped by business, list date and a hash of the normalised classes; never ticks anything), `Reapplied`, `LICENSE_CHANGE_KIND` and `license_change_words` (English and Spanish). Not wired yet (D-2593): the daily list import does not call `reapply_license` and no sender handles `businesses.license_change`. Tests: `tests/integration/tests/license_change_reapply.rs` (TD-BIZ-30).

**What T368 built (D-2701, D-3101).** The checklist from the whole holder and the stored category switch (BIZ-049, BIZ-004, BIZ-021, LIC-002, LIC-012, LIC-014 to LIC-016, LIC-019, LIC-027, LIC-046, LIC-047). `app/businesses/src/capability.rs`: `holder_offer` (pure: per stored-on category, a licensed job type pre-ticked when `licensing::api::eligibility_for_request` gives `Card`, an unticked extra on a `HandymanCard` that is not gas, smoke-alarm, sprinkler or permit work, and the no-license extras except those eligibility refuses or keeps for handymen), `offer_for` (reads `holding` and `stored_enabled`), `stored_enabled`; `reapply_license` offers only stored-on categories. `app/businesses/src/signup.rs`: `stored_category_list`. The served sign-up (T302) calls `offer_for` and `stored_category_list`; `sign_up` still checks the chosen codes against the file. Tests: `tests/integration/tests/capability_offer.rs` (TD-BIZ-46, TD-LIC-27).

**What T157 built (D-2661 to D-2665).** The homeowner's backup choice at the pick (PICK-046, PICK-047, PICK-020; D-400, D-446, D-491, D-492). `migrations/0153_picks_backup_choice.sql`: `picks.backup_choice` (job, `for_quote`, `backup_quote` or NULL for "No backup", `browser` (SHA256 of the page's CSRF token), `pick_id` once bound after her code, `set_at`; append-only, the pick's latest bound row counts) and `picks.backup_move` (once per ended pick, the new pick when it moved; append-only); `picks.pick.end_reason` also allows `ineligible`. `app/picks/src/backup_choice.rs`: `choose_backup` (keeps only another available quote, else "No backup"), the pure `backup_target` (still available, never the ended quote or business), `backup_choice(tx, pick, paused, (actor, at))` (`Backup::Moved(new pick)`, `NoBackup` for PICK-046's text, `Done`), which runs only on a pick ended without an acceptance (expired, cancelled other than replaced or paused, or `payments`' `payment_failed`/`needs_card` with `ended_at`; a job still in Payment pending is refused with nothing recorded) with no other pick waiting and no pause (`Done`), uses the pick's latest bound choice made before the end, and places the backup through `page_pick`, so it gets its fee, audit row and pick notice (PICK-037) and its own window (an emergency job's 60 minutes restart from the move); `backup_text` (en, es, from the brand file). `confirm_reminders::lapse` expires a waiting pick and calls it; `confirm::refused_at_confirm` does the same for an ended window and cancels an account or license refusal as `ineligible` first. `portals`' review step lists the other available quotes in arrival order with "No backup" selected, the final Pick stores the choice before the code step and `bind_backup` binds it to the pick only after her code passed (D-2661); pressing Pick again on the waiting quote with a different backup needs a new code. Not wired yet (D-2663): `run_confirm_ends`, the Confirm press, "Can't take it" (T214), leaving (T228) and the payments paths (T062) call `lapse`, `refused_at_confirm` or `backup_choice`, and the senders of `backup_text` and `lapse_text`. Tests: `tests/integration/tests/backup_choice.rs` (TD-PICK-32).

**What T374 built (D-2712, D-2741, D-2742, D-2743, D-2744).** One read of a business's licensing holder and the sign-up writers for its other-agency licenses (LIC-006, LIC-011, LIC-012, LIC-015, LIC-016, LIC-019, LIC-047, LIC-048, BIZ-049, CARD-001). `app/licensing/src/holder.rs`: `record_pest_attestation` (`self_attested`, number and the wood-destroying and weed-control ticks) and `record_agency_license` (inspector, alarm or septage hauler, `agency_record`; the pest agency or a blank or over-64-character number is `BadRecord`) insert one `licensing.business_license` row per business and agency (a second is `AlreadyRecorded` from the unique index) with one `licensing.license_recorded` audit row whose field is the agency code, never the number; `holder_for(conn, business, HolderInputs)` reads the rows (a row with `paused_at` reads as a reported attestation), the Active classes on the latest ROC list (an Inactive class is never held, D-2743) for the business's latest daily check's number or, before any check, the given ROC number (D-2744; no list or no number is no class; the holder never replaces `roc_check_allows_card`), and the city-list standing from the caller's names (`city_standing`; no names is `Pending`), and returns `HolderParts` (`classes`, `holder` with `roc_covers` false; `for_job` sets it through `ClassMap::covers`). `app/businesses/src/holding.rs`: `holding(conn, id, Known)` adds the handyman standing (`handyman` in `businesses.signup.trades`, `HANDYMAN_CATEGORY`) and calls `holder_for`; no business table holds a license number or name, so the caller passes them (D-2741). Not wired yet: T302 calls the writers on the sign-up POST; T251 and T368 read `holding`. Tests: `tests/integration/tests/holder_read.rs` (TD-LIC-29).

**What T209 built (D-2783 to D-2787).** The optional "Auto-confirm and pay if picked" box and its one off-session charge (PICK-048, PICK-056, FEE-014, SEC-038; D-401, D-480, D-1758). `migrations/0156_quotes_auto_confirm.sql`: `quotes.auto_confirm` (each tick of a quote's box: member, fee shown, authorization version, saved card `pm_…`, `ticked_at`, `unticked_at` with reason `business` or `void`; at most one live tick per quote; the app role may only stamp the untick) and `quotes.stripe_written_answer` (the owner's filed record: date, reference, `allows`, `filed_at`; the latest counts; insert and select only; T108's settings page files it; kept in `quotes`, not `catalog`, because T209 owns only this migration, D-2783), read by `quotes::api::stripe_answer_filed`. `app/quotes/src/auto_confirm_box.rs`: `tick_auto_confirm`, `untick_auto_confirm`, `auto_confirm_of`, `auto_confirm_authorization` (PICK-048's words with the brand file's legal name), `AUTO_CONFIRM_VERSION`; `quotes` never decides whether the box shows. `app/portals/src/business_actions.rs`: `auto_confirm_box` (shown only to the owner or a "Confirm and billing" member, while the answer is filed, with a saved card and one fee), `quote_form_page_full` (the box after the fee lines, unticked unless ticked now) and `set_auto_confirm` (a tick needs `require_step_up`, SEC-038, logged on refusal; an untick needs only the role); `AUTO_CONFIRM_ROUTE` is served by T306. `app/payments/src/off_session.rs`: `OffSessionStripe` (the off-session confirm; T132 adds the adapter call), `saved_card_of`, `charge_off_session`, the only code that charges without a press: the caller's `answer_filed`, card unchanged, then `confirm_and_pay` itself through a crate-private wrapper whose confirm is off-session, so the fee fixed at the pick, the FEE-017 checks, the pause and `begin_attempt`'s hard monthly-limit reservation all apply (D-1758); the press ID is the pick's ID (one attempt per pick, key `pick-<id>-try<n>`); a decline or a bank approval ends the card-fix window it opened, so the pick falls back to the normal Confirm step and window. `app/picks/src/auto_confirm.rs`: the pure `auto_confirm_plan` (no text pick, PICK-056; filed; ticked; a fee to charge; then the void conditions: author removed or without "Confirm and billing", card changed, fee or version changed) and `auto_confirm(pool, stripe, secret, (pick, proof, emergency), at)`, which charges, or voids the tick and queues one `picks.auto_confirm_voided` event (IDs and reason) per pick; the business's notice built from it waits on a template (D-2785). Not wired yet (D-2786): the page-pick and portal-pick routes calling `auto_confirm` after the pick commits, and the pick notice on a successful auto-confirm. Tests: `tests/integration/tests/auto_confirm.rs` (TD-FEE-30, TD-FEE-31, TD-SEC-17 auto-confirm case), `app/picks/src/tests/prop_no_other_off_session_charge.rs` (TD-FEE-30 property and the source scan).

**What T337 built (D-2713, D-2714).** A $0 release is bound to the pick's own business and job (FEE-012, FEE-013, FEE-045, PICK-022; sixth audit U-02, D-2302). `payments::api::no_fee_release(conn, pick, (business, job))` keeps its signature but now reads the pick's `payments.pick_fee` row (fixed at the pick by `fix_fee_at_pick`, D-1871) first: no row, or a caller pair other than the row's, is `ReleaseError::NoFeeDecision` before the ledger is read; then the pick must have no PaymentIntent and a $0 `free-first-win`, `repeat-customer` or `fee-free-job-type` ledger entry for the row's business and job. A waiver for one job of a business therefore never clears a pick of another job. T254's release sink and T255's re-clear inherit the binding. Tests: `tests/integration/tests/no_fee_release_binding.rs` (TD-FEE-57); `confirm_and_pay.rs` and `app/server/tests/release_recovery.rs` gained only the fixture lines that give their picks the stored pair they already pass (D-2832, D-2896, D-2714).

**What T327 built (D-2849 to D-2853).** Task-kind completeness and the no-handler alert (JOB-006, OPS-004; fifth audit AH-001, D-2285; IC-27). `server::worker_registry(settings, stream, (texts, confirm), pairs)` in `app/server/src/lib.rs` is now the whole registry the `worker` binary runs: `register_handlers` plus the worker-only registrations (`privacy`'s photo delete, `messaging.paused_safety`, the hourly no-handler check, and with the Twilio settings the day-31 body delete and `picks.line_retry`); `worker.rs` only builds its inputs. `tests/integration/tests/task_registry.rs` (TD-OPS-36) compiles `app/server/src/lib.rs` in place (the layer table forbids a test crate depending on `askhava-server`, D-736; this compiles `release_minter` and `session_minter` into the test crate, a logged exception to the `MINTS` intent, D-2853), builds that registry from stand-in settings, scans non-test `app/` code for every `const *_KIND: &str` and every literal given to `NewTask::new` (less three subject kinds), requires a floor of known kinds, and fails on a kind neither registered nor listed with its owner task and reason in `tests/task_kinds_deferred.json` (shrink-only; a registered entry prints as stale). `app/server/src/no_handler_alert.rs` (TD-OPS-37): `ops.no_handler_check`, an hourly `core.task` (each run queues the next under the hour's dedupe key; `worker.rs`'s `schedule_start` seeds the first), reads `core::api::dead_without_handler` (kinds and counts of dead letters whose last error is `NO_HANDLER`) and, in test and live only, raises one High `core.task_no_handler` alert per kind whose alert is not open (`ops::api::raise_alert_once`: the open check and the raise in one transaction under an advisory lock on the kind and subject, so checks at once raise one alert, D-2852); the alert's subject kind is the task kind and its subject ID the first 16 bytes of the kind's SHA256 digest; the count is on the alert's error log line, since `ops.alert` has no detail column (D-2850). `core` raises nothing itself.

**What T164 built (D-2897 to D-2901).** One quote format per job type, the notes check and the business's edit or withdrawal before a pick (PICK-051 to PICK-053, REC-010; D-441, D-442, D-446, D-2333, D-2912). `app/quotes/src/formats.rs`: `format_for` (the job type's own `job_type_formats` entry, else `firm` for a recurring category's one-time job type, else the category's `quote_format`), `PriceFields` and `parse_price` (the form's price fields for that one format; a range job reads only low and high, so no visit fee; a per-visit price needs a `VisitSchedule`, one of the five frequencies `payments::rules::Period` converts, and "Other: enter the monthly amount" makes it a monthly price with no schedule), `visit_schedule_of`. `migrations/0157_quotes_formats.sql`: `quotes.quote.visit_schedule`, kept only beside period `visit` and cleared by the `quote_schedule_resets` trigger on every upsert (so an edit through `submit_quote` alone keeps no stale schedule; `save_quote` sets the new one after). `app/quotes/src/notes.rs`: `check_note` (300 characters, the card gate's contact check, the `OFF_PLATFORM` list plus "outside <brand>") and `screen_note` with the caller's `NoteScreener` while AI is on, run before the quote's transaction; `NoteProblem::message` is the reason. `app/quotes/src/edit_withdraw.rs`: `save_quote` and `withdraw_quote` each take the job's row lock first through `jobs::api::lock_for_quote_change` (`SELECT ... FOR UPDATE`), then re-read the status and `models::edit_facts` under it, so neither commits after a pick's move; an edit keeps `received_at`, a withdrawal sets `withdrawn_at` and never deletes. `app/portals/src/business_actions.rs`: `quote_form_page_priced` (`PriceForm`: the format's plain definition, its fields, the schedule, the notes box and their errors) and `job_details_page_with_quote` (`QuoteControls`: "Change your quote", "Withdraw your quote", "Send a new quote", the closed line). The routes are T306's; the homeowner's "Updated <time>" and "Withdrawn by the business" are T384's; the `ai` crate's `NoteScreener` is not built yet. Tests: `tests/integration/tests/quote_formats.rs`, `tests/integration/tests/quote_notes.rs`.

**What T384 built (D-2987 to D-2990).** The homeowner side of PICK-051. `quotes::api::page_quotes` returns every confirmed quote in arrival order, a withdrawn one with `withdrawn`, always `unavailable` and with an empty `original` (the SQL never selects its words), and `updated_at` from `edited_at`; `PageQuote::updated_line` and `updated_stamp` feed `app/portals/templates/quote_page.html` ("Withdrawn by the business" in place, no Pick button; "Updated <time>" in Arizona time and `data-updated`), and `app/portals/static/quote_page.ts` swaps a card whose `data-updated` changed. `quotes::models::list_rows` returns `ListRow` (`Pickable`, `Unavailable`, `Withdrawn`) in page order and `notify::list_texts` numbers LIST lines by page number, skips unavailable ones, writes the withdrawn line and sends nothing when no line is pickable. `quotes::api::shown_card` and `ShownCard` add "Updated <time>" to the card for both readers (`notify::updated_line`). Test: `tests/integration/tests/quote_shown_changes.rs`.

**What T214 built (D-2992 to D-2994).** The homeowner's share box at the pick and "Can't take it" (PICK-049, PICK-050, A11Y-011, A11Y-012; D-443, D-446, D-492, D-3024, D-3032). `migrations/0158_picks_share_box.sql`: `picks.share_consent` (job, `for_quote`, `browser` = SHA256 of the CSRF token, `pick_id` once bound, `set_at`; append-only) and `picks.pick.end_reason` `cant_take`. `app/picks/src/share_consent.rs`: `tick_share` (the ticked final Pick, unbound), `share_ticked`, `bind_share` (with the pick, in the code step's store transaction beside `bind_backup`). `app/portals/src/pick_flow.rs` and `templates/pick_review.html`: the box (`id="share" name="share" value="yes"`, never rendered checked) before the final Pick; an unticked or missing `share` shows the review again with a focused error summary linking to `#share`, `#share-error` before the button, the posted backup kept, and sends and stores nothing, with or without the Verify keys; `store` stores no pick for a quote this browser never ticked (the TAKEN line). `app/picks/src/cant_take.rs`: `cant_take_link` (`https://<domain>/b/picks/{pick}/cant-take`, the pick ID only), carried as `values.cant_take` by `run_pick_notice` (T386 adds the email line); `cant_take_job` and `cant_take` end the waiting pick as `cancelled`/`cant_take` with no fee and move it to her backup through `backup_choice`, else return the job to Quotes ready. `app/portals/src/business_actions.rs`: `cant_take_page` and `cant_take_pick` (owner or "Confirm and billing" of the picked business only); the route is T306's, the homeowner's PICK-047 or PICK-046 text waits on its sender (D-2663). Test: `tests/integration/tests/share_and_cant_take.rs`.

**What T165 built (D-3033 to D-3037).** The business's own names, license numbers and Google and Yelp links at sign-up, and the links on every quote card (BIZ-022, BIZ-052, PICK-041, SEC-041, LIC-011; D-439, D-2943, D-2954). `migrations/0160_businesses_links.sql`: `businesses.business_name` (one row per business: `business_name`, `trade_name`, `city_license_number`; no owner name, phone, email or address) and `businesses.review_link` (`google_url`, `yelp_url`), each with an explicit `GRANT SELECT, INSERT, UPDATE` and no DELETE. `app/businesses/src/links.rs`: `screen_names` and `save_business_name` (screened by `screen_public_name`, written or replaced with a field-name audit row; T302's sign-up POST calls it, T316 and T385 read the row), `business_names`; `on_site` (only Business Profile and Yelp page shapes, no phone number or link inside, D-3037), `check_links` over a `LinkOpener` (production `GuardedOpener`, which fetches only through `adapters::api::fetch_untrusted` under `ReviewSite::policy`), `save_review_links`, `review_links`. `quotes::api::review_lines` makes the plain "Google" and "Yelp" link lines; `quote_card`'s read appends them after the license lines. `portals::business_signup` adds the form fields and `Screen::FormAgain` with `Entered`. License numbers stay in `licensing` (`check_license`, `record_agency_license`); the form only collects them. Tests: `tests/integration/tests/business_links.rs`.

**What T228 built (D-3044 to D-3047).** Leaving Ask Hava and the optional reason (BIZ-051, BIZ-054; D-446, D-463, D-491). `migrations/0161_businesses_leaving.sql`: `businesses.leave_reason` (one row per business: `asked_by` `left` or `paused`, one of the six choice codes or none, the "Something else" words only beside that choice, at most 500 characters; append-only). `app/businesses/src/leaving.rs`: `LeaveReason` (`ALL`, "Busy for a while" first), `ReasonAnswer::parse`, `record_reason` (once per business), `reason_asked`, `pause_ask_due` (paused by itself, `PauseReason::SelfPaused`, strictly more than `PAUSE_ASK_AFTER_DAYS`; D-3048), `leave` (`removed` with its audit row). `app/picks/src/business_left.rs`: `waiting_picks_of`, `end_for_leaving` (`cancelled`/`ineligible`, no fee, then `backup_choice`; Quotes ready when no pick waits). `app/quotes/src/business_left.rs`: `withdraw_for_leaving` (every unwithdrawn quote under its job's row lock, whatever the job's status, D-3048). `payments::api::remove_saved_cards`. `app/portals/src/business_left.rs` with `templates/business_leave.html`: `leave_page` (`LeaveScreen`), `leave` (owner, step-up, one transaction for all of the above plus the end of the business's sessions and unused sign-in links, then `CardDetach` per card after the commit; `Left`, `LeaveError`), `pause_reason`. Routes, the Stripe detach client, the sign-in refusal of a removed business and the digest line wait (D-3045). Test: `tests/integration/tests/business_leaving.rs`.

**What T316 built (D-2923).** One read of each business's public name for the LIST reply and the who's-calling text (PICK-041, BIZ-022, TXT-031, TXT-027; D-336, D-2206, D-2943). `app/businesses/src/public_name.rs`: `businesses::api::public_names(conn, &[Uuid])` returns `(business ID, name)` pairs in the order asked: the trade name (DBA) from `businesses.business_name` when stored, else the business name, screened again by `screen_public_name`. A known business with no row, or whose chosen name fails the screen, is left out and raises one open `businesses.public_name_unusable` alert (`Severity::Normal`, business ID only, `ops::api::raise_alert_once`); an unknown ID is left out with none. Read only; nothing through `licensing`. The callers are T317 (LIST), T318 (who's calling) and IC-71 (quote cards); a portal edit of the name is not built. Test: `tests/integration/tests/business_public_name.rs` (TD-BIZ-43).

**What T333 built (D-2971, D-2972).** A succeeded refund is booked at what Stripe reports (FEE-026, FEE-028, FEE-053; sixth audit U-05, D-2303, D-3018). `app/payments/src/refund_stripe.rs`: `Refund::amount_against(charged)` gives a `RefundAmount` (`Full`; `Differs(cents)` for another `usd` amount; `Foreign` for any other currency; `Missing` for no amount, an amount not above zero, or no currency), the one check the request path, the webhook, the check task, `refund_in_full` and T334's reconcile use. `refunds.rs`: a `succeeded` answer whose amount is `Missing` is re-read once with `fetch_refund` before the recording transaction; `succeeded` books `Full` at the charge, `Differs` at Stripe's amount with one High `payments.refund_amount_mismatch` alert, `Foreign` and `Missing` at the charge with one High `payments.refund_amount_unconfirmed` alert (each through `ops::api::raise_alert_once`, subject the pick); `refund_succeeded(pool, &Refund, now)` takes the caller's re-read (`routes.rs`' `refund_event`, `run_refund_check`) and records nothing for another PaymentIntent; a reversal reverses the amount of the pick's latest `refund` entry. `refund_in_full(pool, stripe, pick)` answers `false` only when the ledger holds the refund at another amount or a re-read reports another currency or `usd` amount, `true` on no succeeded refund, a missing amount or currency, or `NotSent`, and `Stripe` on any other read error. `picks`' `decide_at_96_hours` asks it before the close: an error closes nothing; `false` closes the job, the line and the homeowner's text but queues no `unreachable_refunded` email. Tests: `tests/integration/tests/refund_amounts.rs` (TD-FEE-59).

**What T388 built (D-3074 to D-3078).** The homeowner's copy of the quote she picked, after the Confirm (PICK-042, PICK-054, PICK-011; D-244, D-466, D-493; split from T232 by D-3055). `app/picks/src/settle.rs`: in `SettleStep::Move` (once per pick, under its row lock) `settle` queues one `picks.accepted_quote` task (`picks::api::ACCEPTED_QUOTE_KIND`; payload the pick, job and business IDs only; dedupe `picks.accepted_quote.<pick>`) in the caller's transaction, so `Was` and `Left` queue nothing; `picks::api::accepted_pick(db, job)` reads the job's unended `paid` or `free` pick and its business. `quotes::api::accepted_quote(db, (job, business))` returns an `AcceptedQuote` (page number, stored `Price`, earliest visit day, the business's words) through `page_quotes`, `quoted_prices` and `earliest_visit`, worded by `kind`, `amount` (the LIST text's amount words, kept in step with `notify::amount`) and `visit`, in English or Spanish. `app/intake/src/accepted_quote.rs`: `intake::api::run_accepted_quote(pool, Option<Arc<AcceptedQuoteSender>>, task)` checks the pick is still the job's accepted one, reads her contact and language (`job_homeowner`), the quote and the request number, and calls `messaging::api::queue_accepted_quote`; anything missing sends nothing and the task ends done. `app/messaging/src/accepted_quote_email.rs`: `AcceptedQuoteSender` (email key secret, checked postal line, vault), `render_accepted_quote` (inside `email_layout.html`, homeowner footer, English and draft Spanish, the "Add Ask Hava to your contacts" line with the brand's name and domain, no link and no reward) and `queue_accepted_quote` (reads her email on file only to see that one exists, then one `email_outbox` row keyed on template, contact and pick; `send_email` with `EmailTo::Homeowner` reads the address at send time). `server::register_handlers` registers the kind with no sender until the footer postal setting exists (D-3077), so nothing is emailed in a deployment yet. `app/portals/src/quote_page.rs`: `load` returns `ShownPage` (the unchanged `PageView`, read through `Deref`, plus the accepted quote), and `templates/quote_page.html` shows "You picked quote #<n>" with type, amount, earliest visit, the business's words and the contact card above the list while the token works (D-3078). Test: `tests/integration/tests/accepted_quote_email.rs` (TD-PICK-42).

**What T232 built (D-3069 to D-3072).** The Confirm review step's own pieces, called by T261's `GET` and `POST /b/picks/{pick}/confirm` (no route serves them yet). `migrations/0159_businesses_confirm_terms.sql` widens `businesses.terms_acceptance`'s channel to `business_signup` and `confirm_page` and adds a nullable `pick_id` (a picks record by ID only, no cross-schema foreign key), set exactly on `confirm_page` rows and unique where set; the table stays append-only. `app/businesses/src/confirm_terms.rs`: `businesses::api::record_confirm_terms(conn, (business, person), pick, ConfirmTerms, now)` stores one row (document `business_terms`, version, SHA256 of the published text, language, the assent line `confirm_assent_line(n)` the page shows) in the caller's transaction; a second press for the pick stores nothing (TERMS-033). `app/portals/src/confirm_pay.rs`: `open_confirm_page` stamps the first opening of the person's own pick notice through `messaging::api::record_first_open`, once (OPS-037); `confirm_code_needed` is T199's `fresh_code_needed` over `inbox_shared` (SEC-027); `ConfirmCodes` (Verify, `CodeAddress`, key: the parts `DeviceCodes` is built from), `send_confirm_code` (after `admit_code_send`, Verify by text to the person's own phone only, never the outbox) and `check_confirm_code` (after `admit_code_try`, no `portals.code_try` row; Verify's own 5-check cap ends the code); `confirm_page_with_code` adds the code step (`CodeStep`: "Text me a code" posting to `/b/picks/{pick}/confirm/code`, served by T261, and the code field with its error inside the Confirm form). "Can't take it" stays T214's one page and action, linked from every Confirm page with a form. Test: `tests/integration/tests/confirm_terms.rs` (TD-PICK-43 Confirm half, TD-TERMS-11, TD-SEC-32).

**What T313 built (D-3095 to D-3098).** EMERGENCY card notices go at night with their own words, and the business-notice and receipt senders run in the worker (CARD-020, CARD-021, QH-004, QH-005, BIZ-037, FEE-031; fourth audit F42 and F43, fifth audit AH-001; D-1826 (1), D-2203, D-2287). `app/messaging/src/business_notices.rs`: the parsed notice reads `emergency`, `subject` and `first_line`; an `emergency: true` notice skips the night hold (`quotes::queue_emergency_card` already split who gets it at once and queued the rest for 8 a.m.) and its email carries CARD-021's subject and opens with its first line; one missing either word is malformed and sends nothing; every other notice is held as before. `run_business_notice(pool, Arc<NoticeWorker<C>>, task)` is the handler: `load_lines` (T279's stored modes, no configured business number yet, D-492) and `business_notice` at the worker clock's now in one transaction. `app/core/src/settings.rs`: `ASKHAVA_POSTAL_ADDRESS`, `Settings::postal_address` (the footer's postal line). `app/server/src/lib.rs`: `worker_registry` builds both senders from it (`notice_senders`: a P.O. box or private mailbox line, `ID_SECRET` as the email and version-1 link key; no setting is no registration in dev and CI and a start error naming the key in test and live; any other line is an error naming the key) and registers `messaging.business_notice` (`NoticeChecks`: `businesses::api::may_text`, `picks::api::notice_sent` with the worker's `ConfirmSettings`) and `messaging.charge_receipt` (`run_receipt_email`). Not here: the email-outbox send handler (rows wait in `messaging.email_outbox`, D-1380), T251's call to `queue_emergency_card`, and the accepted-quote and paused-safety senders, which can now take the same setting (D-3098). Tests: `tests/integration/tests/emergency_notices.rs` (TD-CARD-15, TD-FEE-55; compiles `app/server/src/lib.rs` in place, D-3096).

**What T400 built (D-3119 to D-3121).** The `web` process stores the compiled-in catalog file at start-up so a fresh deployment has its `catalog.category` rows (LIC-001, LIC-003): `server::catalog_start::store_catalog(pool)` runs `catalog::api::load_embedded` on one pooled connection, and `main.rs` calls it after the app pool is built and before the listener accepts, on the web path only (never `server migrate` or `server bootstrap-owner`); a failure refuses to start (SEC-002). Loading is safe to repeat and never switches an existing category on, so a restart keeps the owner's switches (LIC-002) and two web processes starting together both succeed. A stored row, on or off, wins; a category with no stored row is off. Nothing here is off-database; recovery is a restart.

## 11. Pinned stack versions

**Policy (D-096, D-453):** the newest stable release published on or before October 14, 2026 (14 days before this plan change), unless cargo-audit or npm audit reports a known vulnerability fixed only in a later release, in which case the fixed release. Versions, dates and licenses were read from the crates.io and npm registry APIs and the Rust release channel in this session on October 28, 2026. T009 writes them into the workspace `Cargo.toml` as exact (`=`) versions and commits `Cargo.lock`; T020 writes `package.json` and commits `package-lock.json` (`BUILD_BRIEF.md:114`). The Python versions this section listed before D-453 now apply only to the tooling, stand-ins and twin (pytest 9.1.1 and PyYAML 6.0.1 in `Ci`).

**Toolchain and platform**

| Component | Version | How verified | Note |
|---|---|---|---|
| Rust | 1.99.0 (2026-10-01), `rust-toolchain.toml` with clippy and rustfmt | Rust release channel; installed and used in this session | 1.99.1 (2026-10-15) is past the cutoff; T009 reads its notes and takes it only for a security fix |
| Edition | 2024 | the scratch workspace built with it | |
| PostgreSQL | 16 (16.15 in the build VM) | `psql --version` | Railway's minor version and the CI service image digest: **verify at T009** |
| Node | 22 (22.23.4 in the build VM) | `node --version` | Only for `tsc`, eslint and Playwright; never in the production image |

**Runtime crates** (each MIT, Apache-2.0, BSD-3-Clause, Unlicense or MPL-2.0; `deny.toml` allows those plus the permissive licenses their dependencies use: MIT-0, ISC, Zlib, Unicode-3.0 and CDLA-Permissive-2.0, and skips the unpublished first-party crates)

|---|---|---|---|

| axum-extra (`cookie`) | 0.12.6 (2026-04-14) | MIT | Signed `__Host-` cookies |
| tokio (`macros`, `rt-multi-thread`, `signal`, `net`, `time`, `sync`) | 1.53.3 (2026-10-13) | MIT | Async runtime; `sync` is named because the worker's semaphore and watch need it and sqlx only happens to enable it (D-491); 1.54.1 is past the cutoff |
| tower / tower-http (`set-header`, `limit`, `fs`, `trace`, `request-id`, `timeout`, `catch-panic`, `sensitive-headers`) | 0.5.3 / 0.7.1 | MIT | Middleware: headers, body limits, static files, tracing, request IDs, timeouts, panic capture (D-481) |
| askama | 0.16.1 (2026-09-04) | MIT OR Apache-2.0 | Compile-time HTML templates, autoescaped |

HEIC is not decoded at launch: the upload form accepts `image/jpeg,image/png,image/webp` so phones convert, and a HEIC upload gets the PRIV-025 reply (D-480, D-481) |
